- **n-plus-one-detector**: n plus one detector specialist. Use when: auditing, validating, and improving n plus one detector workflows and safeguards. ([engremran07/gsmvault](https://github.com/engremran07/gsmvault/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/n-plus-one-detector.agent.md))
- **n-plus-one-detector**: Reviews Java/JPA code (services, repositories, mappers, controllers) for N+1 query antipatterns. Use after entity, service, or repository changes. Discovers remote workflows missing locally (`list --json`, remote-only status) and pulls them (`pull &lt;id&gt;`). Read-only on files (the n8nac CLI writes the pulled .workflow.ts). Used by the mirror-sync workflow that enforces the local-first invariant the edit flow relies on. ([neurawork-git/n8n-autopilot](https://github.com/neurawork-git/n8n-autopilot/blob/HEAD/agents/n8n-mirror.md) · ★18)

HLSL 은 C++ 규칙을 따르되 공유 헤더 함수는 구조체 멤버. 리소스 이름은 소문자. ([sswgame/LearningTemplate](https://github.com/sswgame/LearningTemplate/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/NamingScripts.md) · ★2)

- **nanny**: Rate-limit watchdog spawned by the orchestrator when a background subagent dies with a model rate-limit error. Sleeps until just past the reset window, then finishes — its completion notification is the delayed wake-up that tells the orchestrator to respawn a fresh worker. Runs on the router-selected default subagent model — a different pool than the parent's model — so it can start while the parent's pool is still limited. ([podhmo/gom](https://github.com/podhmo/gom/blob/HEAD/.devin/agents/nanny.md))

- **ndv-optimize**: Performance optimization specialist. Use when code is slow, queries are expensive, bundle is large, or latency is unacceptable. ([emb715/neurodiveragents](https://github.com/emb715/neurodiveragents/blob/HEAD/agents/ndv-optimize.md) · ★2)

- **ndv-research**: Codebase research specialist. Hyperlexic pattern recognition — builds a complete map before synthesizing, finds cross-file relationships that stop being invisible once the whole is held at once. ([emb715/neurodiveragents](https://github.com/emb715/neurodiveragents/blob/HEAD/agents/ndv-research.md) · ★2)
- **ndv-review**: Code review specialist. Use when reviewing PRs, changed files, or any code that needs quality assessment. - **ndv-scope**: Scope enforcer. Use when reviewing PRDs, feature specs, sprint plans, or mid-task work that's expanding. ([emb715/neurodiveragents](https://github.com/emb715/neurodiveragents/blob/HEAD/agents/ndv-scope.md) · ★2)
- **ndv-secure**: Security auditor. Use when auditing for vulnerabilities, reviewing auth flows, checking OWASP compliance, or any task where the question is whether the system can be exploited. ([emb715/neurodiveragents](https://github.com/emb715/neurodiveragents/blob/HEAD/agents/ndv-secure.md) · ★2)
- **ndv-signal**: Metrics skeptic. Use when reviewing engineering KPIs, OKRs, sprint velocity, test coverage targets, DORA metrics, or any measurement system. Audits whether metrics measure what they claim to measure. Goodhart's Law as a cognitive style — the moment a measure becomes a target, it stops being a measure, and Signal notices before anyone else does. ([emb715/neurodiveragents](https://github.com/emb715/neurodiveragents/blob/HEAD/agents/ndv-signal.md) · ★2)
- **ndv-telemetry**: Observability specialist. Use when adding logging, metrics, distributed tracing, or health checks. - **nestjs-debugger**: NestJS의 버그, 예외, 실패하는 테스트, 예상과 다른 동작의 근본 원인을 증거 기반으로 추적할 때 사용한다. 코드를 수정하지 않는 read-only 분석 전담이다. 재현 → 가설 → 검증 → 최소 수정안 제시까지 담당하고, 실제 수정은 nestjs-tdd-implementer가 재현 테스트와 함께 수행한다. "버그 원인 분석", "이거 왜 이렇게 동작해", "예외 추적", "테스트가 왜 실패해" 같은 요청에 위임한다. ([whuk/claude-code-base](https://github.com/whuk/claude-code-base/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/nestjs-debugger.md))
- **nestjs-developer**: Especialista en NestJS (módulos, controllers, providers, DTOs con class-validator, guards, interceptors, TypeORM/Prisma, Swagger). Úsalo para construir, extender o refactorizar APIs en NestJS o integrar LangChain.js en un servicio Nest. ([joseAlfaro25/customs_agents](https://github.com/joseAlfaro25/customs_agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/backend/agents/nestjs-developer.md))
- **nestjs-domain-designer**: NestJS 기능을 구현하기 전에 DDD Rich Domain 모델과 모듈/계층 배치를 설계할 때 사용한다. Entity/Value Object 식별, Finder/Service 프로바이더 구조, Repository 도구 선택(TypeORM/Prisma/SQL-first(Kysely)), Command/Query 흐름 결정을 담당하는 read-only 설계 전담이다. 코드를 작성하지 않고 설계안을 산출하며, 구현은 nestjs-tdd-implementer가 이어받는다. "도메인 설계", "이 기능 어떻게 모델링", "모듈 구조 잡아줘" 같은 요청에 위임한다. ([whuk/claude-code-base](https://github.com/whuk/claude-code-base/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/nestjs-domain-designer.md))
- **nestjs-dto-generator**: Agent for generating NestJS DTOs with validation ([Fujigo-Software/f5-framework-claude](https://github.com/Fujigo-Software/f5-framework-claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/f5-stacks/stacks/backend/nestjs/agents/dto-generator.md) · ★24 · 2 copies)
